- **Jobs-to-be-Done**: **The core idea.** People do not buy products; they hire them to make progress in a specific circumstance. Christensen's well-known example is the morning commuter who hires a milkshake for a long, boring drive, competing with bagels and bananas rather than with other milkshakes. ([DrNabeelKhan/maxim](https://github.com/DrNabeelKhan/maxim/tree/HEAD/composable-skills/frameworks/jobs-to-be-done) · ★2)
